# Seed 35 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - dead code out, two flag genes (Iteration 114)

`seed 35 tag2 6h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --cpus 2,8`,
commit 9b57a35 (Iteration 113: every engine linked with --gc-sections, the
genes alignfn and noinls), started 14:40: four workers (cpus 2, 6, 4, 8),
population 256, 98 generations before the deadline, 25,189 designs; 1,301
refused by the reach limit, no other death. Session 30 measured it with seeds
28, 30, 32 and 33 (calibration 0.998; cpu 2 0.998, cpu 8 0.992) - 660
designs, 2 h 4 min even at 6 rounds: hence --frontier (Iteration 114).

**The stopping rule: small** - the second in a row since sizes last changed.

| objective | seed 35's best | before (seeds 28-33, this session) | improvement | bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| fastest | 0.133 (e5193642ef) | 0.135 | +1.5% | 5% |
| smallest binary + image | 20,318 (585376cf49) | 20,334 | +0.08% | 2% |
| least memory | 186 KB | 188 KB | 2 KB, inside the tie | 8 KB |

The front of all runs: **151** - 121 of seed 35, 23 of seed 33, 7 of seed
32; 51 by memory alone. The fastest e5193642ef 0.133 at 34,840 bytes; the
smallest 585376cf49 20,318 bytes (1.234) - ~2 KB below seed 33's, the dead
code gone. Q (information): log-range best cc7ba38c22 (0.368 at 21,758
bytes, 192 KB).

**The new genes taken up at once**: alignfn on 99 of the run's 178 front
designs, noinls on 100; on the front of all runs 62 and 65 of 151. jitx 9 of
178, the JIT 39, hotinl 107, opbody 159, t2wide 141.

## The run's report

# Evolved VM designs

25189 designs evaluated, 23888 alive. Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, parse, corpus, loop,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
